    $(document).ready(function () {
        $('blockquote.signature.restore').attr('data-content', '+ مشاهده امضاء +');

        $('blockquote.signature.restore').click(function () {
            if ($(this).attr('data-content') == "+ بستن امضاء +") {
                $(this).toggleClass('open').attr('data-content', '+ مشاهده امضاء +');
            } else if ($(this).attr('data-content') == "+ مشاهده امضاء +") {
                $(this).toggleClass('open').attr('data-content', '+ بستن امضاء +');
            }
        });
    });
</script>

<script>
    var slideIndex = 1;

    function plusSlides(n) {
        slideIndex = slideIndex - 1;
        showSlides(slideIndex += n);
    }

    function showSlides(n) {
        var i;

        if (n > 3) {
            slideIndex = 1
        }
        if (n < 1) {
            slideIndex = 3
        }
        for (i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
            $(".mySlides:nth-of-type(" + i + ")").fadeOut(800);
        }
        $(".mySlides:nth-of-type(" + (slideIndex) + ")").fadeIn(800);
        slideIndex++;
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        showSlides();
    });
    $(function () {
        setInterval("showSlides(slideIndex)", 7000);
    });
</script>

        });
    });

<script>
    function lightBox() {
        $('.attachments img:not([src^="/images/ads"] , [src^="/images"]), blockquote.postcontent img:not([src^="/images/ads"] , [src^="/images"],	[src^="images/"])').each(function () {
            var a = $('<a>').attr('href', $(this).attr('src')).attr('data-lightbox', 'yes').attr('data-title', 'irantk.ir');
            $(this).after(a);
            a.append($(this));
        });
    }
    function setIdForVideo() {
        var videoId = 1;
        $('.video-js').each(function() {
            this.id = 'vide_'+videoId;
            videojs(this.id);
            videoId++;
        });
    }
    function resetVideoJs() {
        $('.video-js').each(function() {            
          var video = document.getElementById(this.id);
          videojs(video);
        });
    }
    $(document).ready(function () {
        lightBox();
	setIdForVideo();
    });

  $(function () {
    $('#destforumid').searchableSelect();
  });

</script>
